theorem RiffleUpperV2.Conditional.remainingCommonEdges_le_self : ∀ {k n L : Nat}
  (cuts :
    RiffleUpperV2.Cuts k n
      (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1)))))
  (D : Nat) (g ξ : Real) (p : Fin k) (d : RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.Direction) (i : Nat)
  (hp :
    @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE
      (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHPow.{0, 0} Real Real Real.instPow)
        (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast n) ξ)
      (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast (@RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.pileSize k n L cuts p)))
  (ω :
    Prod.{0, 0}
      (@RiffleUpperV2.Sample k n
        (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
        cuts)
      (@RiffleUpperV2.Sample k n
        (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
          (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
        cuts)),
  @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE
    (@RiffleUpperV2.conditionalExpect.{0}
      (Prod.{0, 0}
        (@RiffleUpperV2.Sample k n
          (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
          cuts)
        (@RiffleUpperV2.Sample k n
          (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
          cuts))
      (@instFintypeProd.{0, 0}
        (@RiffleUpperV2.Sample k n
          (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
          cuts)
        (@RiffleUpperV2.Sample k n
          (@HAdd.hAdd.{0, 0, 0} Nat Nat Nat (@instHAdd.{0} Nat instAddNat) L
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
          cuts)

theorem RiffleUpperV2.Conditional.window_power_le : ∀ {k N : Nat} {ρ : Real}
  (hk : @LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) k)
  (hN : @LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) N)
  (hρ : @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0) (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real Real.instZero)) ρ),
  @LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE
    (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Real Nat Real
      (@instHPow.{0, 0} Real Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Real (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Real Real.instMonoid)))
      (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k) (RiffleUpperV2.Exploration.windowLength N ρ))
    (@HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHMul.{0} Real Real.instMul)
      (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Real Nat Real
        (@instHPow.{0, 0} Real Nat (@NPow.toPow.{0} Real (@Monoid.toNPow.{0} Real Real.instMonoid)))
        (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k) (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 2) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 2))))
      (@HPow.hPow.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHPow.{0, 0} Real Real Real.instPow)
        (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast N)
        (@HMul.hMul.{0, 0, 0} Real Real Real (@instHMul.{0} Real Real.instMul) ρ
          (Real.log (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k))))) :=
fun {k N : ⋯} {ρ : ⋯} (hk : ⋯) (hN : ⋯) (hρ : ⋯) =>
  have hk1 : ⋯ :=
    @cast.{0} (@LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) k)
      (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1) (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real Real.instOne))
        (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Prop
        (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1) (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real Real.instOne))
          (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k))
        (@LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) k)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Prop
          (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1) (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real Real.instOne))
            (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k))
          (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE
            (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
            (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k))
          (@LE.le.{0} Nat instLENat (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) k)
          (@congrFun'.{1, 1} Real Prop
            (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1) (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real Real.instOne)))
            (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE
              (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1)))))
            (@congrArg.{1, 1} Real (Real → Prop)
              (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1) (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real Real.instOne))
              (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
              (@LE.le.{0} Real Real.instLE)
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Real
                (@Nat.cast.{0} Real
                  (@AddMonoidWithOne.toNatCast.{0} Real
                    (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} Real (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} Real Real.instRing)))
                  (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))))
                (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 1)
                  (@One.toOfNat1.{0} Real
                    (@AddMonoidWithOne.toOne.{0} Real
                      (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} Real (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} Real Real.instRing)))))
                (@Nat.cast_one.{0} Real
                  (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} Real (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} Real Real.instRing)))))
            (@Nat.cast.{0} Real Real.instNatCast k))
          (@Nat.cast_le._simp_1.{0} Real
            (@AddGroupWithOne.toAddMonoidWithOne.{0} Real (@Ring.toAddGroupWithOne.{0} Real Real.instRing))
            Real.partialOrder
            (@IsOrderedAddMonoid.toAddLeftMono.{0} Real Real.instAddCommMonoid Real.instPreorder
              Real.instIsOrderedAddMonoid)
            Real.instZeroLEOneClass
            (@FloorSemiring.instCharZero.{0} Real (@Ring.toSemiring.{0} Real Real.instRing) Real.partialOrder
              (@FloorRing.toFloorSemiring.{0} Real Real.instRing Real.linearOrder Real.instFloorRing))
            (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Nat (nat_lit 1) (instOfNatNat (nat_lit 1))) k)))
      hk;
  have hk0 : ⋯ :=
    @Iff.mpr
      (@LT.lt.{0} Real (@Preorder.toLT.{0} Real (@PartialOrder.toPreorder.{0} Real Real.partialOrder))
        (@OfNat.ofNat.{0} Real (nat_lit 0)
          (@Zero.toOfNat0.{0} Real
            (@AddZero.toZero.{0} Real
              (@AddZeroClass.toAddZero.{0} Real
